phirmasta1 irbesartanum
, this document is a summary of the European Public Assessment Report (EPAR) for Ifirmarsta.
explains how the Committee on Medicinal Products for Human Use (CHMP) assessed this medicine to develop its opinion on the granting of the authorisation and recommendations on the conditions of use of Ifirmarsta.
, what is Ifirmast?
in Ifirmasta is a medicine that contains the active substance irbesartan.
is available in tablets (75, 150 and 300 mg).
is the product of Ifirmasta.
, the medicine is similar to ‘reference medicine’ which is already registered in the European Union, namely Aprovel.
, more information on generies, see the relevant document and answers here.
, what is Imarrsta used for?
medicine is used in adults with essential hypertension (in shock blood pressure).
, the "scient" means that hypertension has no obvious cause.
in Ifirmasta is also used to treat kidney disease in adults with hypertension and diabetes type 2.
